# XPU Ops Bug Scrub Report

- **Repository**: `intel/torch-xpu-ops`
- **Generated**: 2026-07-01 (cutoff for Section 6: 2026-06-24)
- **Total issues in workbook**: 333
- **Classified (non-empty `AR`)**: 333
- **Empty `AR` (no verdict)**: 0

## 1. Summary

This report groups the 333 tracked torch-xpu-ops issues by the `AR` (Action Required) column in the workbook. An issue may appear in multiple AR buckets if its `AR` cell contains more than one value (joined with `; `). Cross-cutting slices (duplicated issues, external dependency blockers, newly filed issues, stale requests) are listed separately for visibility.

**Headline counts (multi-membership — an issue with N AR values is counted N times):**

| AR Bucket | Issues |
|---|---:|
| Close/Skip | 7 |
| Need Owner | 67 |
| Land PR | 81 |
| Wait for PR | 13 |
| Need Response | 208 |
| Need check case existence | 0 |
| Verify | 30 |
| UNCLASSIFIED | 0 |
| Duplicated | 27 |
| External dependency (non-upstream-pytorch, non-SYCL-kernel) | 58 |
| Upstream-pytorch | 4 |
| CPU fallback | 4 |
| Filed within last 7 days | 25 |
| Requests pending > 1 week | 52 |

## 3. Action Required (by AR bucket)

_[↑ Back to Index](#sec-2)_

Issues are grouped by the `AR` column from the Issues sheet. Each issue appears in every AR bucket it lists. Rows inside each bucket are split by `Category` (existing taxonomy column); rows within a category table are sorted by `Priority` (P0 → P3).

Issues whose `Dependency` is a third-party blocker (`oneDNN` / `oneMKL` / `oneAPI` / `triton` / `driver` / `xccl`) are hidden here and listed only under §5 Dependency, except when their AR includes `Land PR` or `Wait for PR` (a live next action makes the row actionable).
